# envoy-go Roadmap

## Schema

Phases are tracked as rows in the tables below. Columns:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `id` | Phase identifier (e.g. `00`, `04`, `04.1`). Sub-phases use a decimal suffix (see §6 of `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md`). |
| `title` | Short human-readable title matching the phase directory slug under `docs/envoy-go/phases/`. |
| `depends-on` | Comma-separated list of phase ids that must be `done` before this phase can enter `in-progress`. Empty for the first phase. |
| `status` | One of `planned`, `in-progress`, `blocked`, `done`. |
| `sub-phases` | If the phase was split (see §6), this lists the sub-phase ids (e.g. `04.1, 04.2`). Empty otherwise. |
| `summary` | One-line summary of the phase's differential surface at completion. |

### Invariants

- **Append-only history.** Rows are never deleted. Only `status` and `sub-phases` columns are updated in place. Sub-phases get their own rows.
- **Status transitions.** `planned` → `in-progress` → (`blocked` or `done`). `blocked` is an exit state only when an upstream dependency is unavailable; the phase resumes on the same row when unblocked.
- **Sub-phase rows.** When a parent phase is split, the parent stays `in-progress` with `sub-phases` listing children; each child gets its own row whose `depends-on` references the prior child (so children execute in order unless stated otherwise).
- **Families (§9 headings) are not rows.** Each heading is expanded into per-phase rows when a phase under that family enters `in-progress`. Do not pre-populate rows for unbrainstormed phases.

---

## MVP Trunk (phases 00–08)

These phases ship in order. Each depends on the previous being `done`, because each adds a primitive the next phase relies on. Splitting (§6) is permitted within any phase.

| 09 | http-filter-fault | 08 | done |  | New `internal/filter/http/fault/` package implementing `envoy.filters.http.fault` (Envoy v1.37.2 canonical fault filter) under the 07.1 framework; first concrete phase under §9 HTTP filters family. MVP envelope per SPEC §1 (revised per §11 empirical pins): `delay.percentage` + `delay.fixed_delay` (async-resume via `time.AfterFunc` + `cb.ContinueDecoding`); `abort.percentage` + `abort.http_status` constrained `[200, 600)` per PGV pin §11.1 (terminal-replace via `cb.SendLocalReply` with body byte-exact `fault filter abort` (18 bytes, no trailing newline) + 4-header set per §11.3); `headers` field for request-header gating with StringMatcher.exact only per §11.8; `max_active_faults` concurrency cap (LBP-1 sixth application). Differential fixture `0011-http-fault` green (4 scenarios per SPEC §7.1: delay-only listener-inherited, combined delay+abort per-route, per-route wholesale-override, headers-field exact-match gate; the BRAINSTORM-anticipated 5th "header-driven abort" scenario drops per §11.5 amendment). FIRST production filter exercising async-resume on the request side (cors only short-circuited via SendLocalReply). Deferred per `BOOTSTRAP_PROMPT.md` §9 family-expansion: `response_rate_limit`, `abort.grpc_status` (gRPC family), all four runtime-key fields (Runtime + hot restart family), **`delay.header_delay` + `abort.header_abort` proto sub-messages COUPLED with the four `x-envoy-fault-{delay,abort}-request[-percentage]` request headers** (deferred together per §11.5 + ADR-0104 — the request-header path requires the proto sub-messages to activate; cannot be cleanly separated), `upstream_cluster`, `downstream_nodes`, `disable_downstream_cluster_stats`, `filter_enabled` / `filter_enabled_runtime`, HeaderMatcher non-exact variants (each gets a deferral ADR per ADR-0040 format). 5 stats per §11.6 (4 counters + 1 gauge; 17→22-name BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT extension; route A — `fault.response_rl_injected` permanently-zero counter for parity). Anticipated ADRs ADR-0100..ADR-0107 (per BRAINSTORM §9; ADR-0104 repurposed from implementation to deferral). Per ADR-0106 (BRAINSTORM Decision 12), HTTP filters family expands as flat top-level rows (no parent 09 row with sub-phases); each filter is its own coherent phase. ADR-0045 surface-split release valve stays available if SPEC/PLAN find > ~1500 LoC / > ~25 tasks. |

| 11 | http-filter-local-ratelimit | 10 | done |  | New `internal/filter/http/localratelimit/` package implementing `envoy.filters.http.local_ratelimit` (Envoy v1.37.2 canonical local rate-limiting filter) under the 07.1 framework. FOURTH §9 family-row (after cors @ 07.1, fault @ 09, header_mutation @ 10). MVP envelope per SPEC §1.1: 5 fields consumed (`stat_prefix`, `token_bucket{max_tokens, tokens_per_fill, fill_interval}`, `status{code}` default 429, plus the `LocalRateLimitPerRoute` per-route container); 14 fields silently ignored under inline deferral (BRAINSTORM-anticipated ADR-0120 omnibus dropped per SPEC §8.1; deferral organized by 8 family-clusters at SPEC §2.1 + §13.5: descriptor-action; Runtime + shadow-mode; xDS cluster-state; response-side header injection; per-connection lifecycle; multi-stage; X-RateLimit headers + vh policy; gRPC trailer). Token-bucket primitive Option A — lazy refill on access, single sync.Mutex per bucket, no per-bucket goroutine, monotonic time via `time.Now().UnixNano()` (Go ≥1.9 monotonic guarantee). FIRST production filter where per-route TPFC override implies independent stateful resources (per-route entries each own a `tokenBucket` per ADR-0117 = ADR-0073 amendment); cors / fault / header_mutation per-route is data-only. Differential fixture `0013-http-local-ratelimit` (4 scenarios per SPEC §7.1: basic-allow, basic-rate-limited, refill-after-fill_interval ±10ms, per-route-override). Stat surface 22→26 names (4 new counters; `enforced == rate_limited` MVP invariant per ADR-0118; filter-specific Prometheus tag-extractor `envoy_local_http_ratelimit_prefix` per SPEC §11.5 — corrects BRAINSTORM-hypothesized `envoy_http_conn_manager_prefix`). Rate-limited response status 429, body byte-exact `local_rate_limited` (18 bytes; no LF), 4-header set lowercase wire-form (`content-length`, `content-type`, `date`, `server: envoy` — `server: envoy` corrects BRAINSTORM-hypothesized `envoy-go`); SendLocalReply mechanism mirrors fault abort. Empirical pin §11.2c: `fill_interval` 50ms minimum is filter-internal Envoy check (NOT PGV); envoy-go `New` factory mirrors. Empirical pin §11.2/§11.4: `filter_enabled` + `filter_enforced` `RuntimeFractionalPercent` fields default to 0% in reference Envoy; fixture configs MUST set both to 100% explicitly on BOTH sides for differential equivalence (envoy-go silent-ignores; behavior is always-100%). NO new framework primitive required (unlike phase 10's `ResolveAllTiers`); reuses existing 3-tier `PerRouteConfig.Resolve`. 15th fuzzer `FuzzLocalRateLimitConfigParse`. Anticipated ADRs ADR-0114..ADR-0119 (6 ADRs per SPEC §8; ADR-0120 omnibus dropped per §8.1; ADR-0114 captures package-name divergence from header_mutation's underscore-preserving directory pattern — phase 11 uses `localratelimit/` no-underscore directory). Per ADR-0106 (validated at phases 09 + 10 + 11), §9 family-rows are flat top-level rows; phase 11 lands as row `11`, NOT as a sub-phase of any §9 parent. ADR-0045 surface-split release valve stays available if PLAN finds > ~1500 LoC / > ~25 tasks; SPEC's position is single-row at ~1100–1400 LoC / ~12–16 tasks. |
| 12 | http-filter-csrf | 11 | done |  | New `internal/filter/http/csrf/` package implementing `envoy.filters.http.csrf` (Envoy v1.37.2 canonical same-origin enforcement filter) under the 07.1 framework. FIFTH §9 family-row (after cors @ 07.1, fault @ 09, header_mutation @ 10, local_ratelimit @ 11). MVP envelope per SPEC §1.1 (revised per §11 empirical pins): 1 field actively consumed (`additional_origins[].StringMatcher.exact` only — non-exact variants dropped at PARSE time per ADR-0101 §3); 1 field PGV-validated-not-honored (`filter_enabled` — REQUIRED at parse-time per §11.11 amendment, MAJOR REVISION from BRAINSTORM "silent-ignore" hypothesis; runtime always-100%-active); 1 field silent-ignored entirely (`shadow_enabled` — optional at parse, always-never-shadow at runtime; couples to Runtime + hot restart family). Method gate `{POST, PUT, DELETE, PATCH}` confirmed (§11.1). Source-origin extraction trichotomy per §11.2 amendment (MAJOR REVISION from BRAINSTORM 2-case hypothesis): (i) `Origin: null` literal → empty source NO Referer fallback; (ii) `Origin:` empty/absent → fall back to Referer's hostAndPort; (iii) `Origin:` non-empty unparseable → verbatim string used as source. Comparison algorithm HOST:PORT-only equality per §11.3 + §11.7 + §11.8 amendments (MAJOR REVISION from BRAINSTORM "full-origin with normalization" hypothesis): scheme STRIPPED on both sides (X-Forwarded-Proto irrelevant); NO case folding; NO default-port stripping; trailing slash stripped via URL parser; `additional_origins[].exact` matches `host[:port]` form NOT full URL with scheme (operator footgun documented in BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT §13.1 + §13.4). Rejection wire shape confirmed: `SendLocalReply(403)` + body byte-exact `Invalid origin` (14 bytes, no LF) + 4-header set lowercase wire-form (`content-length: 14`, `content-type: text/plain`, `date: <RFC1123>`, `server: envoy`); reuses fault.abort/local_ratelimit primitive (§11.10). Per-route TPFC wholesale-override per ADR-0073 — data-only AND **stats SHARED with listener-level** (per §11.9 amendment; diverges from phase 11 local_ratelimit precedent which had independent per-route stats per ADR-0117). NO ADR-0073 amendment required. NO new framework primitive (thinnest §9 family-row to date). Stat surface 26→29 names (3 new counters per HCM stat_prefix: `request_valid`, `request_invalid`, `missing_source_origin`; `shadow_request_invalid` dropped from MVP — confirmed Envoy v1.37.2 also doesn't emit it under all-defaults config per §11.6; shadow-only mode reuses the regular 3-counter family); namespace anchors at HCM stat_prefix (reuses existing `envoy_http_conn_manager_prefix` Prometheus tag-extraction per §11.6 — NO new SN flattening rule; UNLIKE phase 11). Differential fixture `0014-http-csrf` (6 scenarios per SPEC §7.1: same-origin allowed, cross-origin rejected, additional_origins host:port match, missing source-origin rejected, Referer fallback, per-route wholesale override with shared stats; GET passthrough unit-only). Fixture configs MUST set `filter_enabled.default_value: 100/HUNDRED` explicitly on both sides per §11.11 amendment (PGV-required by Envoy at parse-time). 16th fuzzer `FuzzCsrfPolicyConfigParse`. Anticipated ADRs ADR-0120..ADR-0124 (5 ADRs per SPEC §8; no consolidation; no ADR-0073 amendment paragraph; no ADR-0061 amendment). Per ADR-0106 (validated at phases 09 + 10 + 11 + 12), §9 family-rows are flat top-level rows; phase 12 lands as row `12`, NOT as a sub-phase of any §9 parent. ADR-0045 surface-split release valve stays available if PLAN finds > ~1500 LoC / > ~25 tasks; SPEC's position is single-row at ~750-1100 LoC / ~10-14 tasks. |
| 13 | http-filter-buffer | 12 | done | 2026-05-09 | New `internal/filter/http/buffer/` package implementing `envoy.filters.http.buffer` (Envoy v1.37.2 canonical "buffer entire request body before forwarding upstream" filter) under the 07.1 framework. SIXTH §9 family-row (after cors @ 07.1, fault @ 09, header_mutation @ 10, local_ratelimit @ 11, csrf @ 12). 1 listener-level field consumed (`max_request_bytes` UInt32Value; envoy-go-own validation: non-nil + value > 0 + value ≤ 1048576 / 1 MiB per ADR-0126). FIRST §9 family-row to interact with the framework's body-buffering machinery; FIRST §9 family-row whose per-route proto introduces a top-level `disabled` boolean shortcut as a first-class shape — codifies "disabled-OR-override" as the **5th canonical per-route discipline** (ADR-0125). Body-counting algorithm — STREAMING-CAP ONLY (per ADR-0127 v2 + SPEC §11.6 empirical refutation of Content-Length fast-fail): `DecodeHeaders` returns `Continue` on bodied + non-disabled (NOT `StopIteration` — envoy-go synchronous-HCM dispatch constraint per ADR-0128); `DecodeData` returns `DataContinue` mid-stream (NOT `DataStopIterationAndBuffer`; HCM's `connection.go` bodyBuf already accumulates all bytes per ADR-0076 + ADR-0128); overflow fires `SendLocalReply(413, "Payload Too Large", connClose)` + `DataStopIterationNoBuffer`; terminal chunk invokes `maybeAddContentLength` (chunked → fixed-CL conversion observable upstream, mirrors `buffer_filter.cc:91-97`). Wire shape byte-equivalent to reference Envoy on every observable axis. Per-route stats SHARED with listener-level (mirrors phase-12 csrf ADR-0124; DIVERGES from phase-11 local_ratelimit ADR-0117). Stat surface stays at 29 names (phase 13 contributes ZERO new stat-table entries per SPEC §1.1 amendment 5 — reference Envoy emits no `envoy_http_buffer_*` counter family; overflow observable via existing `downstream_rq_4xx`). 6-scenario differential fixture `0015-http-buffer`. 17th fuzzer `FuzzBufferConfigParse`. 4 ADRs: ADR-0125 (package + 5th canonical per-route), ADR-0126 (compiledConfig + parse-time validation), ADR-0127 v2 (body-counting algorithm + maybeAddContentLength + 413 wire reuse; in-place updated at Task 12 to retract 100-Continue addendum + reflect Continue/DataContinue landed algorithm), ADR-0128 (NEW: framework primitives at `internal/filter/hcm/connection.go` — synthetic empty-terminal RunDecodeData + post-body CL reconciliation; FIRST framework deltas since phase 07.1). Phase 13 retires the SPEC §4 "no framework deltas" invariant. Per ADR-0106, §9 family-rows are flat top-level rows; phase 13 lands as row `13`. |
| 14 | http-filter-compressor | 13 | done | 2026-05-10 | New `internal/filter/http/compressor/` package implementing `envoy.filters.http.compressor` (Envoy v1.37.2 canonical response-side compression filter, gzip-only MVP) under the 07.1 framework. SEVENTH §9 family-row (after cors @ 07.1, fault @ 09, header_mutation @ 10, local_ratelimit @ 11, csrf @ 12, buffer @ 13). MVP envelope shipped per phase 14 SPEC §1 + §1.1 amendments: 6 listener consumed + 9 listener silent-ignored + 2 codec (Gzip) consumed + 3 codec silent-ignored = 8 grand-total consumed + 12 silent-ignored + 1 parse-rejected (non-Gzip `compressor_library` TypeURLs per ADR-0130). Body algorithm Path B (buffer-then-compress; framework as-is) per ADR-0131 — operates on `resp.Body` materialized by HCM's encode-chain at `connection.go:467-475` (one-shot full-body `RunEncodeData(ctx, resp.Body, true)`); writes back via new framework primitive `EncoderFilterCallbacks.OverwriteBody(b []byte)` per ADR-0131 §Decision (vi) (~20-25 LoC framework delta at `internal/filter/http/{callbacks.go, chain.go}` + `internal/filter/hcm/{connection.go, h2dispatch.go}`; symmetric to phase-13 ADR-0128 decode-side primitives); wire-shape divergence (envoy-go: `Content-Encoding: gzip` + fixed `Content-Length: <gzipped>` + identity transfer; Envoy: streaming `Content-Encoding: gzip` + chunked transfer + no CL) accepted with explicit forward-pointer to future encode-side streaming framework phase. Per-route TPFC `disabled`-OR-`overrides.response_direction_config` shape — SECOND row using ADR-0125 5th canonical disabled-OR-override (after phase-13 buffer); per-route override surface is FILTER-SPECIFIC and NARROWER than the listener-level config (only `remove_accept_encoding_header` per `ResponseDirectionOverrides` proto + per-route library swap silent-ignored); ADR-0125 gained amendment §(viii)-(x) at SPEC commit; no new per-route ADR. Stat surface 29→46 names (17 new counters per HCM stat_prefix: `header_compressor_overshadowed`, `header_compressor_used`, `header_identity`, `header_not_valid`, `header_wildcard`, `no_accept_header`, `not_compressed_etag`, `response.{compressed, content_length_too_small, not_compressed, total_compressed_bytes, total_uncompressed_bytes}`, `request.{compressed, content_length_too_small, not_compressed, total_compressed_bytes, total_uncompressed_bytes}`); NO new SN flattening rule (uses existing SN2 per phase 14 SPEC §1.1 amendment 3; the BRAINSTORM-hypothesized Rule SN10 was refuted at SPEC time when empirical scrape revealed the existing HCM-anchored SN2 sufficient). ## Feature Families (09+)

Each heading below becomes one or more brainstormed phases when it enters `in-progress`. Do **not** pre-populate per-phase rows here; add them at brainstorming time. Splitting (§6) applies as it would to trunk phases.

### HTTP filters family

Header manipulation, cors, compression, fault, local + global rate limit, jwt_authn, rbac, ext_authz, ext_proc, oauth2, csrf, buffer, lua, wasm, adaptive concurrency, admission control, bandwidth limit.

### Network filters family

**FAMILY CLOSED (2026-06-10).** (echo, direct_response, sni_cluster, rbac_network, zookeeper — DONE via phases 26/27/28; mongo — DONE via phase 29; kafka_broker — DONE via phase 31; redis — DONE via phase 32; thrift — DONE via phase 33.) After phase 33 phase-done, 0 candidates remain — the §9 Network-filters family CLOSES; the next phase opens a NEW feature family. (Historical: after phase 31, 2 candidates {redis, thrift} remained; each needed its own brainstorm-time risk assessment — both terminal-proxy surfaces needing routing/pooling seams of their own.) **`kafka_broker` SELECTED as the next §9 subject at the phase-30 BRAINSTORM (2026-06-06)** — its contrib risk is CONFIRMED (contrib-only image absent from the standard reference pin; canonical v3 proto in the `/contrib` go module not yet a dep), so it lands via TWO phases: the **phase-30 reference-image pin-refresh** to `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` (the infra prerequisite, in-progress; SPEC done — the phase-30 SPEC's D30-1 AMEND-D30 corrected the image from the BRAINSTORM-assumed `envoyproxy/envoy-contrib:v1.37.2`, whose repo has no v1.37.x tag, to the `contrib-`-prefixed tag in the standard repo) THEN **phase 31 = `kafka_broker`** (the §9 row proper). **`kafka_broker` IN-PROGRESS / landing at phase 31** — its BRAINSTORM landed 2026-06-07 (`docs/envoy-go/phases/31-network-filter-kafka-broker/BRAINSTORM.md`): a HEADER-ONLY decode envelope + FULL per-API-key `request.<key>`/`response.<key>` both-direction counter parity (via a `correlation_id`→`api_key` per-connection map) under `kafka.<stat_prefix>.`; the four active features parse-accepted-behavior-deferred (the broker-address-rewrite write-mutation flagged as a future surgery sub-phase); the project's FIRST `/contrib v1.32.4` go.mod dep (added with its first consumer); a SINGLE unsplit row + a pre-authorized 31.1/31.2 split axis; cross-side `StatsAsserter` + a new BackendKind + the 40th fuzzer; ADR-0228 anticipated. **After phase 31 phase-done, 2 §9 candidates remain: {redis, thrift}** — both terminal-proxy surfaces (command routing + upstream pooling) needing routing/pooling seams of their own, each its own future brainstorm; they carry forward unbrainstormed. **`redis_proxy` DONE at phase 32 (phase-done 2026-06-09; BRAINSTORM 2026-06-08, `docs/envoy-go/phases/32-network-filter-redis-proxy/`)** — chosen over `thrift_proxy` from {redis, thrift} (RESP is far leaner than Thrift's transport×protocol matrix + method-routing + nested thrift-filter sub-chain); the project's **FIRST terminal routing proxy** (parses RESP, routes `catch_all` to one upstream cluster, pools/pipelines upstream connections itself — there is NO passive-observe MVP for a terminal proxy). A SINGLE-ROUTE-TERMINAL MVP (RESP codec + catch_all single-cluster routing + a basic per-host upstream connection pool with FIFO/positional correlation + the full single-route command set [the EAGER 180-command roster] + per-command/downstream/upstream stats under `redis.<stat_prefix>.` via a SINGLE-label-hoist prom arm; sharding/redirection/fragmentation/AUTH/faults/mirroring/read_policy/custom_commands deferred). Landed the framework's **SIXTH structural extension** — the upstream connection-pool / cluster-routing seam (framework-level but redis-scoped, YAGNI; thrift reuses it later) — as a **2-way FEATURE-PROGRESSIVE seam-first pre-split** (parent row 32 + 32.1 [seam + RESP codec + round-trip, IMPL done 2026-06-08] + 32.2 [commands + stats + matrix, IMPL done 2026-06-09]); 2 ADRs (ADR-0229 filter + ADR-0230 seam, both ACCEPTED — bodies landed in-place per ADR-0044; DECISIONS tail STAYS ADR-0230, next-free ADR-0231). Final counts: stat surface **1091**, fixtures **58**, fuzzers **41** (`FuzzRESPDecode`), BackendKind tail **32** (`TCPRedisResponder`). The FIRST §9 row whose downstream RESPONSE bytes are the load-bearing differential proof (redis_proxy generates them). **After phase 32 phase-done, 1 §9 candidate remained: {thrift}** — a terminal-proxy surface (transport×protocol matrix + method-routing + a nested thrift-filter sub-chain) REUSING the 32.1 upstream-pool seam (ADR-0230, the anticipated second consumer). **`thrift_proxy` DONE at phase 33 (phase-done 2026-06-10; BRAINSTORM 2026-06-09, `docs/envoy-go/phases/33-network-filter-thrift-proxy/`)** — the project's SECOND terminal routing proxy (after redis_proxy) and the LAST §9 candidate. A SINGLE-PAIR SINGLE-ROUTE-TERMINAL MVP (the Thrift message-begin codec for ONE transport×protocol pair under `payload_passthrough` + a match-all/single-method `route_config` route to one cluster + a router-only nested sub-chain + the global `thrift.<stat_prefix>.request*/response*` stats); the ADR-0230 upstream-pool seam REUSED UNCHANGED (the FIRST reuse of a prior framework seam → framework-ZERO-touch; seq_id parse-validated but single-flight positional correlation, NOT demux-load-bearing); a SINGLE FLAT ROW (the kafka-31 precedent — no new seam → no seam-first split driver; ~800–1300 LoC / ~15–22 tasks under the ADR-0045 gate; a pre-authorized 33.1/33.2 escape-valve unconsumed); the §9 SECOND row whose downstream RESPONSE bytes are the load-bearing differential proof (after redis-32 — thrift_proxy generates them) + cross-side `StatsAsserter` + a new `TCPThriftResponder` BackendKind + the 42nd fuzzer `FuzzThriftDecode`; 1 ADR (ADR-0231 — filter only; no parent-umbrella, no seam ADR). **DEP-FRAMING CORRECTION (BRAINSTORM finding):** thrift_proxy is a **CORE `/envoy v1.32.4` extension** (verified in the go module cache — `thrift_proxy/v3` under `go-control-plane/envoy@v1.32.4`, where `redis_proxy/v3` lives; NOT under `/contrib` where `kafka_broker/v3` lives), REFUTING the earlier "`/contrib` like kafka_broker" framing — anticipated ZERO new go.mod dep because the existing CORE `/envoy` dep already carries it (the redis D32-1 posture), and it boots on the contrib reference image because thrift is a core extension present in both the standard and contrib images (the project standardized on contrib at ADR-0227). The deferred surface (the rest of the 3×3 matrix / full route_config richness / the non-router thrift_filters / full struct parsing / seq_id-keyed multiplexing / the close-direction counters) carries forward. Final counts: stat surface **1116** (+25: 24 counters + 1 gauge `request_active`), fixtures **60** (`0057-thrift-roundtrip` + `0058-thrift-boot-reject`), fuzzers **42** (`FuzzThriftDecode`), BackendKind tail **33** (`TCPThriftResponder`); 1 ADR (ADR-0231 — filter only, ACCEPTED; DECISIONS tail STAYS ADR-0231, next-free ADR-0232). The §9 SECOND row whose downstream RESPONSE bytes are the load-bearing differential proof (after redis-32). The FIRST row to REUSE a prior framework seam UNCHANGED (the ADR-0230 upstream-pool seam → framework-ZERO-touch, validating its redis-scoped YAGNI sizing). **After phase 33 phase-done (2026-06-10), 0 §9 candidates remain — the §9 Network-filters family CLOSES.** The family that opened at phase 26 (chain framework + echo/direct_response/rbac_network) closes at phase 33; the next phase opens a NEW feature family (see the family §§ below).

### Load balancing family

least_request, random, ring_hash, maglev, subset LB, locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds.

**FAMILY OPEN at phase 34 (2026-06-10); STAYS OPEN.** The family OPENED with **`least_request` DONE at phase 34** (`load-balancer-least-request`; IMPL done 2026-06-11 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/34-load-balancer-least-request/PROGRESS.md`): the first LB policy beyond the phase-02 ROUND_ROBIN, carried on the NEW `internal/cluster` LB acquire/release seam (**ADR-0232** ACCEPTED — the project's first framework seam outside `internal/filter/network`; the unexported `loadBalancer` interface reshaped to `Pick() (Endpoint, func(), error)`, OPTION C with the exported `Cluster` surface byte-stable; every future family row reuses it as the durable structural asset) + the un-weighted P2C `leastRequest` policy (**ADR-0233** ACCEPTED). Remaining candidates after 34: {random, ring_hash, maglev, subset LB, locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} — each its own future brainstorm; per ADR-0106 sibling rows are NOT pre-populated. The ADR-0232 seam already accommodates them (random/ring_hash/maglev implement the same interface and ignore the active counters at zero cost; future active-load-aware variants reuse the counters).

**`random` DONE at phase 35** (`load-balancer-random`; IMPL done 2026-06-11 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/35-load-balancer-random/PROGRESS.md`): the SECOND family row, picked from the 7-candidate roster as the **zero-churn ADR-0232 seam drop-in** (the stateless non-keyed `randomLB` implements the existing `loadBalancer` interface returning `noopRelease`, ignoring the active counters at zero cost — the **FIRST reuse of the ADR-0232 seam**, validating its "RANDOM drops in at zero cost" §Consequences claim) over the seam-EXTENDING hash policies (RING_HASH/MAGLEV additionally need the ADR-0232 `Pick(hashInput)` widening — DEFERRED to whichever future phase lands a hash policy). `Cluster.LbPolicy RANDOM` (enum value 3, NO config message — zero PGV arm; ONE draw `rng() % n`, no active-count consult, no tie-break — the v1.37.2 `peekOrChoose` mirror); **ADR-0234** ACCEPTED (the policy ONLY — no seam ADR since reuse; ADR-0024 UNAMENDED); the anti-skew `0060-lb-random` fixture (RANDOM stays uniform-band under the `0059` held-conn stimulus — the contrapositive of least_request; the IMPL-re-tuned `{floor 6, ceiling 40}` band — the SPEC's `{12,32}` flaked 2/20, re-tuned to 60/60 flake-free within the pinned PRINCIPLE; the 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks STILL bite); NO new fuzzer (fuzzers STAY 42 — the SECOND no-fuzzer phase) / NO new BackendKind (tail STAYS 33) / ZERO stat-delta (surface STAYS 1116 — the SECOND zero-delta phase; the phase-34 family-level firsts, now ESTABLISHED); fixtures 61 → 62. After phase 35 phase-done, **6** candidates remain: {ring_hash, maglev, subset LB, locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}. Row 35 flipped `in-progress → done` at the phase-35 IMPL six-gate (NO parent rollup per ADR-0106).

**`ring_hash` DONE at phase 36 — both legs LANDED** (`load-balancer-ring-hash`; **36.1 IMPL done 2026-06-12, 36.2 IMPL done 2026-06-13**, SPEC done 2026-06-11 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/36-load-balancer-ring-hash/{BRAINSTORM,SPEC,PLAN,PROGRESS}.md` + `docs/envoy-go/phases/36.2-load-balancer-ring-hash-http/{SPEC,PLAN,PROGRESS}.md`; the D-RH1..D-RH8 empirical pins executed; the FINAL ADR-0045 decision CONSUMED the 36.1/36.2 split — 36.1 [seam + policy + tcp plane] + 36.2 [HTTP route hash_policy plane] both LANDED): the THIRD family row, picked from the 6-candidate roster as the **natural primary-`LbPolicy`-enum completion** (ROUND_ROBIN/LEAST_REQUEST/RANDOM done → RING_HASH/MAGLEV remain among the primary enum values; subset/locality/priority/panic are orthogonal MODIFIERS, several gated by the absent health-checking boundary) and the policy that exercises the **seam-EXTENSION** ADR-0232 §Consequences explicitly anticipated ("the hash-keyed RING_HASH/MAGLEV will additionally require widening `Pick` to receive a request-derived hash key … the next LB phase that lands a hash policy must plan for it"). `Cluster.LbPolicy RING_HASH` (enum value 2, WITH a `Cluster.RingHashLbConfig` message [`minimum_ring_size`/`maximum_ring_size`/`hash_function` XX_HASH·MURMUR_HASH_2] — a config-parse arm + HAND-ROLLED PGV gate, the least_request shape; the first LB-config arm since least_request). The project's **first consistent-hashing policy** — a Ketama ring built once from the endpoint set, `Pick(hashKey)` binary-searches for the first ring position ≥ key (wrap), returns `noopRelease` (no per-pick state, RELEASE half unused). **The seam EXTENDS** (the PICK-INPUT half — contrast random's zero-churn reuse): the unexported `loadBalancer.Pick` widens to receive an optional request-derived hash key carried via `context.Context` (the consumer computes it, `cluster.go` extracts via `withHashKey`/`hashKeyFrom` + threads it), the **exported `Cluster` surface stays BYTE-STABLE** (`Dial`/`AcquireH1` already take `ctx` — the OPTION-C discipline preserved), `PickEndpoint()` no-hash → random fallback, the 3 existing policies ignore the key. **BOTH hash-input planes** (BRAINSTORM Q1): tcp_proxy (`hash_policy: source_ip`) + the HTTP router (route-level `RouteAction.hash_policy`, header + connection-properties source-ip subset; cookie/query/filter-state DEFERRED). **2 ADRs LANDED at the 36.1 IMPL** — **ADR-0235** (the LB hash-key seam extension — the ADR-0232 PICK-INPUT half) + **ADR-0236** (the ring_hash policy), both ACCEPTED in-place per ADR-0044 (DECISIONS tail ADR-0234 → ADR-0236; next-free ADR-0237) — the phase-34 two-ADR (seam + policy) shape (contrast random's single-ADR reuse). The differential is the AFFINITY + SPREAD arm — `0061-lb-ring-hash` (tcp source-IP; 36.1) + `0062-lb-ring-hash-http` (HTTP header; 36.2) binding the client to multiple `127.0.0.x` source IPs (D-RH8): same-key→same-backend (affinity, stable) + distinct-keys→different-backends (spread), PER SIDE [cross-side-exact host identity IFF D-RH2 proves byte-exact ring reproduction feasible; else per-side bands] + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` prong; NO boot-reject dir (the config-parse rejects land unit-level — AMEND-RH5); NO new BackendKind (tail STAYS 33) / NO new fuzzer (fuzzers STAY 42) / **stat surface 1116 → 1119 at the 36.1 IMPL** (+3 `ring_hash_lb.*` gauges — NOT zero-delta, REFUTED at the SPEC; AMEND-RH4). **SPEC refinements (AMEND-RH1..RH8):** cross-side host identity INFEASIBLE in the harness (Docker source-IP NAT + per-side ring layouts) → PER-SIDE deterministic affinity+spread + cross-side byte-equiv + cross-equal stats (AMEND-RH8); the seam is `Pick(hashKey uint64, hasHash bool)` + an exported additive `cluster.WithHashKey` (AMEND-RH7); xxHash64 hand-rolled (~45 LoC; not a dep); the gate mirrors TWO reject layers (PGV `<= 8388608` + runtime `min > max`; AMEND-RH5). **36.1/36.2 by-plane split CONSUMED** (the FINAL ADR-0045 decision — ~490 prod LoC / ~16–20 tasks; consumed on subsystem-coupling + fixture-asymmetry grounds; the phase-34 valve precedent): 36.1 = seam-ext + ringHashLB + xxHash64 + manager + the 3 gauges + tcp_proxy `source_ip` plane + `0061`; 36.2 = HTTP route `hash_policy` plane + `0062`. D-RH1..D-RH8 EXECUTED at the SPEC (live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network`). Row 36 registers `in-progress` (flat family row + the now-CONSUMED 36.1/36.2 split) at the BRAINSTORM commit; it flips `in-progress → done` per-leg at the 36.1 then 36.2 IMPL six-gate(s) (NO parent rollup per ADR-0106) — **the 36.1 leg flipped `in-progress → done` at the 36.1 IMPL six-gate (2026-06-12; fixtures 62 → 63, stat surface 1116 → 1119, DECISIONS tail → ADR-0236); the 36.2 leg flipped `in-progress → done` at the 36.2 IMPL six-gate (2026-06-13; fixtures 63 → 64, stat surface STAYS 1119, DECISIONS tail → ADR-0237 [the HTTP route `hash_policy` PRODUCER plane])**. After phase 36 phase-done, **5** candidates remain: {maglev, subset LB, locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} — maglev the cheap follow-on (it REUSES the phase-36 ADR-0235 hash-input seam + BOTH ADR-0237 producer planes unchanged, building only a different lookup table).

**`maglev` DONE at phase 37** (`load-balancer-maglev`; IMPL done 2026-06-13 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/37-load-balancer-maglev/PROGRESS.md`): the FOURTH family row, picked from the 5-candidate roster as **the cheap follow-on** ADR-0235/ADR-0236 §Consequences explicitly named — it REUSES the ADR-0235 hash-key seam (`Pick(hashKey uint64, hasHash bool)`) + BOTH ADR-0237 producer planes (tcp `source_ip` + HTTP route `hash_policy`) UNCHANGED, swapping the Ketama RING for a fixed-size MAGLEV lookup table behind the SAME `Pick` seam (a new `maglevLB` impl in `internal/cluster/maglev.go` mirroring `ringHashLB` structurally; returns `noopRelease` — the table is built once, no per-pick state). `Cluster.LbPolicy MAGLEV` (enum value 5, WITH a `Cluster.MaglevLbConfig` message [a SINGLE `table_size` field, `UInt64Value`, PGV `value must be less than or equal to 5000011`; NO `hash_function` knob — maglev always uses `xxHash64` internally] — a config-parse arm LIGHTER than ring_hash's three-field arm, on par with least_request's single `choice_count`). The project's **fifth LB policy** and its **second consistent-hashing policy** — the **FIRST family row to REUSE the ADR-0235 seam** (contrast phase-36, which BUILT it; the hash-policy analogue of phase-35 reusing the ADR-0232 release seam). **Key BRAINSTORM decision (Q1):** a configured non-prime `table_size` is **DEPARTURE-rejected** fail-fast at config-build (accept unset→default-65537 / any prime ≤ 5000011 / the cap 5000011; the reference accepts a composite verbatim — a RECORDED DEPARTURE; the maglev permutation requires `M` prime for a terminating full-cycle fill — each `skip ∈ [1, M-1]` is coprime to a prime `M`, so every per-host permutation is a full `M`-cycle reaching every empty slot). **1 ADR anticipated:** ADR-0238 (the maglev policy ONLY — NO seam ADR [ADR-0235 reused], NO producer ADR [ADR-0237 reused]; the phase-35 single-ADR-on-reuse shape). **Proof-shape:** the header-key NAT-transparent TRUE cross-side AFFINITY + SPREAD arm — `0063-lb-maglev` (an HTTP route header `hash_policy` feeding MAGLEV; the `0062` convention reused verbatim, retargeted to a MAGLEV cluster): same-value→same-backend (affinity, the modular invariant, BOTH sides) + distinct-values→`>=2` backends (spread) + cross-side byte-equiv + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` (the anticipated `maglev_lb.*` gauges + `upstream_rq_total` cross-equal); the `X-Hash` header survives Docker source-IP NAT (TRUE cross-side, unlike a tcp source_ip key — `reference_differential_hash_key_cross_side_infeasible`). **Single flat row, NO split** (the producers already exist — no second subsystem to couple; ~120–220 prod LoC / ~8–10 tasks, well under the ADR-0045 gate; the least_request-34/random-35 precedent). **Anticipated moves at the IMPL:** fixtures 64 → 65 (`0063`), DECISIONS tail → ADR-0238, **stat surface 1119 → ~1121** (+2 anticipated `maglev_lb.*` gauges — `min_entries_per_host`/`max_entries_per_host`, no `size` gauge; D-M4 pins the exact surface), fuzzers UNCHANGED 42, BackendKind tail UNCHANGED 33, ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod deps (xxHash64 already hand-rolled). The byte-stable-reject text extends `… RANDOM, RING_HASH` → `…, MAGLEV` (the THREE-site blast radius at SPEC D-M5: `manager.go` + `manager_test.go` [the doubly-hit retarget MAGLEV → CLUSTER_PROVIDED] + `BEHAVIOR_CONTRACT.md`). Counts UNCHANGED at the BRAINSTORM (stat surface 1119 / fixtures 64 / fuzzers 42 / BackendKind tail 33 / DECISIONS tail ADR-0237, next-free ADR-0238). D-M1..D-M6 to be EXECUTED at the SPEC (live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network`). Row 37 registers `in-progress` (flat family row, NO split) at the BRAINSTORM commit; it flips `in-progress → done` at the phase-37 IMPL six-gate (NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). After phase 37 phase-done, **4** candidates remain: {subset LB, locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}.

**`subset` IN-PROGRESS at phase 38** (`load-balancer-subset`; BRAINSTORM done 2026-06-13 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/38-load-balancer-subset/BRAINSTORM.md`): the FIFTH family row and the family's **FIRST wrapper** — `Cluster.lb_subset_config` is NOT a `Cluster.LbPolicy` enum value (unlike the five incumbents roundRobin/leastRequest/randomLB/ringHashLB/maglevLB); it is a wrapper triggered by the presence of `lb_subset_config`, that selects a subset of endpoints matching the route's `metadata_match` and **delegates to the cluster's `lb_policy` as the child policy run within that subset**. Picked from the 4-candidate roster (Q0) as **the only candidate decoupled from the absent health-checking boundary** (locality-weighted / priority / panic all key off host health/availability — the Upstream-robustness family's territory; subset is a pure metadata-match-then-delegate mechanism, orthogonal to health, composing with all five existing policies). **Q1=A — a wrapper `subsetLB` owning precomputed per-subset child LBs, ALL FIVE child policies** (each subset's child built by the existing policy factory; the hashKey passes straight through to a hash-policy child); the LB pick-seam EXTENDS with a SECOND pick-input (the resolved subset match — after ADR-0235's hash key; a seam BUILD → its own ADR), threaded via `WithSubsetMatch`/`subsetMatchFrom`; a NEW `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension (the `envoy.lb` namespace — the FIRST per-endpoint dimension beyond `Host:Port`); a NEW HTTP producer plane (`RouteAction.metadata_match`). **NO lb_policy reject-text change** (subset is not an enum value — the phase-34/35/36/37 doubly-hit reject-text-retarget lineage BREAKS; the reject surface shifts to `lb_subset_config` validation). **Pre-authorized 38.1/38.2 by-plane split (Q2/Q2b — the phase-36 valve):** 38.1 = subset LB on the HTTP `RouteAction.metadata_match` plane; 38.2 = `RouteAction.weighted_clusters` routing + `WeightedCluster.ClusterWeight.metadata_match` (a NEW per-request weighted-cluster-selection primitive — the router resolves a SINGLE cluster per route today; the brainstorm surfaced this hidden prerequisite). **2 ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0239 (the subset-match seam-input extension + the `RouteAction.metadata_match` producer — the seam-BUILD half, parallel to ADR-0235) + ADR-0240 (the subset LB policy + the `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension — the policy half, parallel to ADR-0236/0238); the phase-36 two-ADR shape; §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. **Proof-shape:** the static-config NAT-transparent TRUE cross-side subset-affinity arm — `0064-lb-subset` (HTTP routes each carrying a `metadata_match` pinned to a subset over `envoy.lb`-tagged endpoints): subset affinity (every served backend ∈ the expected subset, BOTH sides — the SET-membership property) + a fallback arm + cross-side byte-equiv + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` (the `lb_subsets_*` stats); NO new BackendKind (tail STAYS 33; the `0063` HTTP backends reused) / anticipated NO new fuzzer (no wire decode; fuzzers anticipated STAY 42). Counts UNCHANGED at the BRAINSTORM (stat surface 1121 / fixtures 65 / fuzzers 42 / BackendKind tail 33 / DECISIONS tail ADR-0238, next-free ADR-0239); anticipated moves at the 38.1 IMPL: fixtures 65 → 66 (`0064`), DECISIONS tail → ADR-0240, **stat surface 1121 → ~1125** (~+4 `lb_subsets_*` stats — D-SS4 pins), fuzzers/BackendKind UNCHANGED (42/33), ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod deps. D-SS1..D-SS7 to be EXECUTED at the SPEC (live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network`). BRAINSTORM: `docs/envoy-go/phases/38-load-balancer-subset/BRAINSTORM.md`. Row 38 registers `in-progress` (flat family row WITH a pre-authorized 38.1/38.2 by-plane split) at the BRAINSTORM commit; the 38.1 leg flips `in-progress → done` at the phase-38.1 IMPL six-gate, then 38.2 (NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). After phase 38 phase-done, **3** candidates remain: {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} — all gated by the absent health-checking boundary (the Upstream-robustness family).

### Upstream robustness family

Active health checks HTTP/TCP/gRPC/custom, outlier detection variants, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, per-protocol connection pooling.

**FAMILY OPEN at phase 39 (2026-06-14)** — the first NEW feature family since §9 (Network filters) closed at phase 33. **`active health checks` IN-PROGRESS at phase 39** (`upstream-health-check`; BRAINSTORM done 2026-06-14 — `docs/envoy-go/phases/39-upstream-health-check/BRAINSTORM.md`): the family keystone — `Cluster.health_checks` (`[]core.v3.HealthCheck` field 8) active health checking over an HTTP + TCP + gRPC roster + the project's FIRST per-host health-state dimension + the project's FIRST cluster-level background runtime (`Manager.StartHealthChecks(ctx)` / `Drain()` lifecycle) + the health-aware LB pick (Approach A: pick-time filter + `healthy_panic_threshold` fallback; the health view build-time-injected, the `Pick` signature byte-stable). It UNBLOCKS the 3 health-gated Load-balancing candidates {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}. Pre-authorized 39.1/39.2 by-codec split: 39.1 = the keystone + HTTP checker (2 ADRs — ADR-0242 state+runtime / ADR-0243 health-aware-pick); 39.2 = TCP + gRPC checkers reusing the 39.1 substrate. Differential `0066-health-check-http` (poll-`/stats`-to-convergence then 100%-live-host assertion; NO new BackendKind/fuzzer at 39.1). Anticipated at the 39.1 IMPL: fixtures 67 → 68 (`0066`), DECISIONS tail ADR-0241 → ADR-0243, stat surface 1125 → ~1131 (~+6 `health_check.*`/`membership_healthy`), fuzzers/BackendKind UNCHANGED (42/33), ZERO new packages/go.mod modules. After phase 39 phase-done, **4** family candidates remain: {outlier detection, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, per-protocol connection pooling}.

### HTTP/3 + QUIC family

quic-go transport, downstream H3 listener, upstream H3 cluster, `h3spec` gate.

### gRPC family

gRPC bridge, gRPC-Web, gRPC-JSON transcoding, interop conformance.

### xDS / dynamic config family

ADS, delta xDS, LDS, CDS, RDS, EDS, SDS, RTDS, reconnection, initial-fetch timeout.

### Observability family

gRPC ALS, OTLP access log, OTel/Zipkin/Jaeger/Datadog/XRay tracing, stats sinks, tap filter.

### Runtime + hot restart family

Runtime layer (RTDS consumer); hot-restart / graceful-drain semantics beyond phase 08's minimum.

### WASM host family

Own multi-phase sub-project: ABI, engine binding, proxy-wasm conformance.

### Deprecated / edge features

Explicit out-of-scope ADRs unless later re-opened.
